\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

Ramesh's pocket money is first reduced by 25% and then increased by 20%.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Final value = Initial value × (1 - Reduction%) × (1 + Increase%)

\n\n

Net percentage change = ((Final value - Initial value) / Initial value) × 100

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the original pocket money be ₹100.

\n\n

Af\nter a 25% reduction:

\n\n

⇒ ₹100 × (1 - 0.25) = ₹100 × 0.75 = ₹75

\n\n

Now, increase the reduced amount by 20%:

\n\n

⇒ ₹75 × (1 + 0.20) = ₹75 × 1.20 = ₹90

\n\n

Now, calculate the net change:

\n\n

⇒ Net percentage change = ((90 - 100) / 100) × 100

\n\n

⇒ Net percentage change = (-10 / 100) × 100

\n\n

⇒ Net percentage change = -10%

\n\n

∴ There is a net decrease of 10% in Ramesh's original pocket money.

दिया गया है:

\n\n

रमेश के जेब खर्च में पहले 25% की कमी की जाती है और फिर 20% की वृद्धि की जाती है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

अंतिम मान = प्रारंभिक मान × (1 - कमी%) × (1 + वृद्धि%)

\n\n

शुद्ध प्रतिशत परिवर्तन = ((अंतिम मान - प्रारंभिक मान) / प्रारंभिक मान) × 100

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि मूल जेब खर्च ₹100 है।

\n\n

25% कमी के बाद:

\n\n

⇒ ₹100 × (1 - 0.25) = ₹100 × 0.75 = ₹75

\n\n

अब, कम हुई राशि में 20% की वृद्धि करते हैं:

\n\n

⇒ ₹75 × (1 + 0.20) = ₹75 × 1.20 = ₹90

\n\n

अब, शुद्ध परिवर्तन की गणना करते हैं:

\n\n

⇒ शुद्ध प्रतिशत परिवर्तन = ((90 - 100) / 100) × 100

\n\n

⇒ शुद्ध प्रतिशत परिवर्तन = (-10 / 100) × 100

\n\n

⇒ शुद्ध प्रतिशत परिवर्तन = -10%

\n\n

∴ रमेश के मूल जेब खर्च में 10% की शुद्ध कमी है।

প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

রমেশের পকেট মানি প্রথমে 25% কমানো হয় এবং তারপর 20% বাড়ানো হয়।

\n\n

ব্যবহৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

চূড়ান্ত মান = প্রাথমিক মান × (1 - হ্রাস%) × (1 + বৃদ্ধি%)

\n\n

মোট শতাংশ পরিবর্তন = ((চূড়ান্ত মান - প্রাথমিক মান) / প্রাথমিক মান) × 100

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

ধরা যাক আসল পকেট মানি 100 টাকা।

\n\n

25% কমানোর পর:

\n\n

⇒ 100 টাকা × (1 - 0.25) = 100 টাকা × 0.75 = 7", "te": "

ఇవ్వబడింది:

రమేష్ పాకెట్ మనీ మొదట 25% తగ్గించి, తరువాత 20% పెంచబడింది.

ఉపయోగించిన ఫార్ములా:

తుది విలువ = ప్రారంభ విలువ × (1 - తగ్గింపు%) × (1 + పెరుగుదల%)

నికర శాతం మార్పు = ((తుది విలువ - ప్రారంభ విలువ) / ప్రారంభ విలువ) × 100

లెక్కింపు:

అసలు పాకెట్ మనీ ₹100 ఉండనివ్వండి.

25% తగ్గింపు తర్వాత:

⇒ ₹100 × (1 - 0.25) = ₹100 × 0.75 = ₹75

ఇప్పుడు, తగ్గించిన మొత్తాన్ని 20% పెంచండి:

⇒ ₹75 × (1 + 0.20) = ₹75 × 1.20 = ₹90

ఇప్పుడు, నికర మార్పును లెక్కించండి:

⇒ నికర శాతం మార్పు = ((90 - 100) / 100) × 100

⇒ నికర శాతం మార్పు = (-10 / 100) × 100

⇒ నికర శాతం మార్పు = -10%

∴ రమేష్ అసలు పాకెట్ మనీలో నికరంగా 10% తగ్గుదల ఉంది.

\n", "mr": "

दिले आहे:

\n\n

रमेशच्या पॉकेटमनीमध्ये प्रथम २५% घट झाली आणि नंतर २०% वाढ झाली.

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

अंतिम मूल्य = प्रारंभिक मूल्य x (१ - घट%) x (१ + वाढ%)

\n\n

निव्वळ टक्केवारी बदल = ((अंतिम मूल्य - प्रारंभिक मूल्य) / प्रारंभिक मूल्य) x १००

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मूळ पॉकेटमनी ₹१०० मानू.

\n\n

२५% घट झाल्यानंतर:

\n\n

⇒ ₹१०० x (१ - ०.२५) = ₹१०० x ०.७५ = ₹७५

\n\n

आता, कमी झालेल्या रकमेमध्ये २०% वाढ करा:

\n\n

⇒ ₹७५ x (१ + ०.२०) = ₹७५ x १.२० = ₹९०

\n\n

आता, निव्वळ बदल मोजा:

\n\n

⇒ निव्वळ टक्केवारी बदल = ((९० - १००) / १००) x १००

\n\n

⇒ निव्वळ टक्केवारी बदल = (-१० / १००) x १००

\n\n

⇒ निव्वळ टक्केवारी बदल = -१०%

\n\n

∴ रमेशच्या मूळ पॉकेटमनीमध्ये निव्वळ १०% घट झाली आहे.

\n", "bn": "

প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

রমেশের পকেট মানি প্রথমে 25% কমানো হয় এবং তারপর 20% বাড়ানো হয়।

\n\n

ব্যবহৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

চূড়ান্ত মান = প্রাথমিক মান × (1 - হ্রাস%) × (1 + বৃদ্ধি%)

\n\n

মোট শতাংশ পরিবর্তন = ((চূড়ান্ত মান - প্রাথমিক মান) / প্রাথমিক মান) × 100

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

ধরা যাক আসল পকেট মানি 100 টাকা।

\n\n

25% কমানোর পর:

\n\n

⇒ 100 টাকা × (1 - 0.25) = 100 টাকা × 0.75 = 75 টাকা 

\n\n

এখন, কমানো পরিমাণ 20% বাড়ান:

\n\n

⇒ 75 টাকা આપેલ:

\n\n

રમેશના ખિસ્સાખર્ચમાં પહેલા 25% ઘટાડો અને પછી 20% વધારો કરવામાં આવે છે.

\n\n

ઉપયોગમાં લેવાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

અંતિમ કિંમત = પ્રારંભિક કિંમત x (1 - ઘટાડો%) x (1 + વધારો%)

\n\n

કુલ ટકાવારી ફેરફાર = ((અંતિમ કિંમત - પ્રારંભિક કિંમત) / પ્રારંભિક કિંમત) x 100

\n\n

ગણતરી:

\n\n

ધારો કે મૂળ ખિસ્સાખર્ચ ₹100 છે.

\n\n

25% ઘટાડો પછી:

\n\n

⇒ ₹100 x (1 - 0.25) = ₹100 x 0.75 = ₹75

\n\n

હવે, ઘટાડેલી રકમમાં 20% વધારો કરો:

\n\n

⇒ ₹75 x (1 + 0.20) = ₹75 x 1.20 = ₹90

\n\n

હવે, કુલ ફેરફારની ગણતરી કરો:

\n\n

⇒ કુલ ટકાવારી ફેરફાર = ((90 - 100) / 100) x 100

\n\n

⇒ કુલ ટકાવારી ફેરફાર = (-10 / 100) x 100

\n\n

⇒ કુલ ટકાવારી ફેરફાર = -10%

\n\n

∴ રમેશના મૂળ ખિસ્સાખર્ચમાં 10% નો કુલ ઘટાડો થયો છે.

\n", "kn": "

ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

ರಮೇಶ್‌ನ ಪಾಕೆಟ್ ಮನಿ ಮೊದಲು 25% ರಷ್ಟು ಕಡಿಮೆಯಾಗಿದೆ ಮತ್ತು ನಂತರ 20% ರಷ್ಟು ಹೆಚ್ಚಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ.

\n\n

ಸೂತ್ರ ಬಳಸಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

ಅಂತಿಮ ಮೌಲ್ಯ = ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಮೌಲ್ಯ x (1 - ಕಡಿತ%) x (1 + ಹೆಚ್ಚಳ%)

\n\n

ನಿವ್ವಳ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು ಬದಲಾವಣೆ = ((ಅಂತಿಮ ಮೌಲ್ಯ - ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಮೌಲ್ಯ) / ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಮೌಲ್ಯ) x 100

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

ಮೂಲ ಪಾಕೆಟ್ ಮನಿ ₹100 ಆಗಿರಲಿ.

\n\n

25% ಕಡಿತದ ನಂತರ:

\n\n

⇒ ₹100 x (1 - 0.25) = ₹100 x 0.75 = ₹75

\n\n

ಈಗ, ಕಡಿಮೆ ಮಾಡಿದ ಮೊತ್ತವನ್ನು 20% ರಷ್ಟು ಹೆಚ್ಚಿಸಿ:

\n\n

⇒ ₹75 x (1 + 0.20) = ₹75 x 1.20 = ₹90

\n\n

ಈಗ, ನಿವ್ವಳ ಬದಲಾವಣೆಯನ್ನು ಲೆಕ್ಕ ಹಾಕಿ:

\n\n

⇒ ನಿವ್ವಳ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು ಬದಲಾವಣೆ = ((90 - 100) / 100) x 100

\n\n

⇒ ನಿವ್ವಳ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು ಬದಲಾವಣೆ = (-10 / 100) x 100

\n\n

⇒ ನಿವ್ವಳ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು ಬದಲಾವಣೆ = -10%

\n\n

∴ ರಮೇಶ್‌ನ ಮೂಲ ಪಾಕೆಟ್ ಮನಿಯಲ್ಲಿ 10% ರಷ್ಟು ನಿವ್ವಳ ಇಳಿಕೆ ಕಂಡುಬಂದಿದೆ.

\n", "ta": "

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n\n

ரமேஷின் கைச்செலவுப்பணம் முதலில் 25% குறைக்கப்பட்டு பின்னர் 20% அதிகரிக்கப்படுகிறது.

\n\n

பயன்படுத்தப்பட்ட சூத்திரம்:

\n\n

இறுதி மதிப்பு = ஆரம்ப மதிப்பு x (1 - குறைப்பு%) x (1 + அதிகரிப்பு%)

\n\n

நிகர சதவீத மாற்றம் = ((இறுதி மதிப்பு - ஆரம்ப மதிப்பு) / ஆரம்ப மதிப்பு) x 100

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

அசல் கைச்செலவுப்பணம் ₹100 என வைத்துக்கொள்வோம்.

\n\n

25% குறைப்புக்குப் பிறகு:

\n\n

⇒ ₹100 x (1 - 0.25) = ₹100 x 0.75 = ₹75

\n\n

இப்போது, குறைக்கப்பட்ட தொகையை 20% அதிகரிக்கவும்:

\n\n

⇒ ₹75 x (1 + 0.20) = ₹75 x 1.20 = ₹90

\n\n

இப்போது, நிகர மாற்றத்தைக் கணக்கிடுங்கள்:

\n\n

⇒ நிகர சதவீத மாற்றம் = ((90 - 100) / 100) x 100

\n\n

⇒ நிகர சதவீத மாற்றம் = (-10 / 100) x 100

\n\n

⇒ நிகர சதவீத மாற்றம் = -10%

\n\n

∴ ரமேஷின் அசல் பைசா பணத்தில் 10% நிகர குறைப்பு உள்ளது.

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ:

\n\n

ਰਮੇਸ਼ ਦੀ ਜੇਬ ਖਰਚੀ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ 25% ਘਟਾਈ ਜਾਂਦੀ ਹੈ ਅਤੇ ਫਿਰ 20% ਵਧਾਈ ਜਾਂਦੀ ਹੈ।

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਫਾਰਮੂਲਾ:

\n\n

ਅੰਤਿਮ ਮੁੱਲ = ਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ ਮੁੱਲ x (1 - ਕਮੀ%) x (1 + ਵਾਧਾ%)

\n\n

ਕੁੱਲ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਤਬਦੀਲੀ = ((ਅੰਤਿਮ ਮੁੱਲ - ਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ ਮੁੱਲ) / ਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ ਮੁੱਲ) x 100

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

ਮੰਨ ਲਓ ਕਿ ਮੂਲ ਜੇਬ ਖਰਚੀ ₹100 ਹੈ।

\n\n

25% ਕਮੀ ਤੋਂ ਬਾਅਦ:

\n\n

⇒ ₹100 x (1 - 0.25) = ₹100 x 0.75 = ₹75

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ ਕੁੱਲ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਤਬਦੀਲੀ = (-10 / 100) x 100

\n\n

\n\n

சேமிக்கிறார், அவரது மாதச் சம்பளம் என்ன?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "₹8,000"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "₹6,000"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "₹7,000"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "₹10,000"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Let total monthly salary be 100%.

\n

Applying successive reductions for the remaining amounts at each step:

\n

Final Saving % = 100% × (60/100) × (50/100) × (70/100) = 21%

\n

Given 21% = ₹1,260

\n

Total Salary (100%) = ₹1,260 × (100/21) = ₹6,000

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹6,000.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Amount spent on food = 40% of salary

\n

Amount spent on transport = 50% of the remaining

\n

Amount spent on clothes = 30% of the remaining

\n

Final monthly savings = ₹1,260

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Final Value = Initial Value × (1 − p1/100) × (1 − p2/100) × (1 − p3/100)

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the total monthly salary be x.

\n

⇒ Amount remaining after spending on food = x × (100 − 40)/100 = 0.60x

\n

⇒ Amount remaining after spending on transport = 0.60x × (100 − 50)/100 = 0.30x

\n

⇒ Amount remaining after spending on clothes (Final Savings) = 0.30x × (100 − 30)/100 = 0.21x

\n

⇒ We are given that the final savings is ₹1,260.

\n

⇒ 0.21x = 1260

\n

⇒ 21x / 100 = 1260

\n

⇒ x = (1260 × 100) ÷ 21

\n

⇒ x = 60 × 100 = 6000

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹6,000.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Successive Percentage Change

\n

When a value undergoes multiple percentage changes sequentially, the final value is found by multiplying the initial base value by a combined multiplying factor of (1 − r/100) for each successive reduction.

\n

Base Reference

\n

In successive reduction questions, the phrase \"of the remaining\" indicates that the new base for the next percentage deduction is simply the remaining balance left after the previous deduction.

दिया गया है:

\n\n

खाने पर खर्च की गई सैलरी का प्रतिशत = 40%

\n\n

शेष सैलरी का परिवहन पर खर्च किया गया प्रतिशत = 50%

\n\n

शेष सैलरी का कपड़ों पर खर्च किया गया प्रतिशत = 30%

\n\n

खर्च के बाद बचत = ₹1,260

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

कुल सैलरी = बचत / (1 - (खाने पर खर्च किया गया प्रतिशत + शेष पर परिवहन पर खर्च किया गया प्रतिशत + शेष पर कपड़ों पर खर्च किया गया प्रतिशत))

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि मासिक सैलरी ₹S है।

\n\n

खाने पर खर्च की गई राशि = S का 40% = 0.40S

\n\n

खाने के बाद शेष सैलरी = S - 0.40S = 0.60S

\n\n

परिवहन पर खर्च की गई राशि = शेष का 50% = 0.50 x 0.60S = 0.30Sఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

ఆహారంపై ఖర్చు చేసిన జీతం శాతం = 40%

\n\n

మిగిలిన జీతంలో రవాణాపై ఖర్చు చేసిన శాతం = 50%

\n\n

మిగిలిన జీతంలో దుస్తులపై ఖర్చు చేసిన శాతం = 30%

\n\n

ఖర్చుల తర్వాత పొదుపు = ₹1,260

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

మొత్తం జీతం = పొదుపు / (1 - (ఆహారంపై ఖర్చు చేసిన శాతం + మిగిలిన దానిలో రవాణాపై ఖర్చు చేసిన శాతం + మిగిలిన దానిలో దుస్తులపై ఖర్చు చేసిన శాతం))

\n\n

లెక్కింపు:

\n\n

నెలవారీ జీతం ₹S అనుకుందాం.

\n\n

ఆహారంపై ఖర్చు చేసిన మొత్తం = S లో 40% = 0.40S

\n\n

ఆహారం తర్వాత మిగిలిన జీతం = S - 0.40S = 0.60S

\n\n

రవాణాపై ఖర్చు చేసిన మొత్తం = మిగిలిన దానిలో 50% = 0.50 x 0.60S = 0.30S

\n\n

రవాణా తర్వాత మిగిలిన జీతం = 0.60S - 0.30S = 0.30S

\n\n

దుస్తులపై ఖర్చు చేసిన మొత్తం = మిగిలిన దానిలో 30% = 0.30 x 0.30S = 0.09S

\n\n

దుస్తుల తర్వాత మిగిలిన జీతం = 0.30S - 0.09S = 0.21S

\n\n

పొదుపు = ₹1,260

\n\n

కాబట్టి, 0.21S = 1,260

\n\n

⇒ S = 1,260 / 0.21

\n\n

⇒ S = 6,000

\n\n

నెలవారీ జీతం ₹6,000.

\n\n

\n\n

বেতনের খাদ্যদ্রব্যে ব্যয়ের শতাংশ = 40%

\n\n

বাকি বেতনের পরিবহনে ব্যয়ের শতাংশ = 50%

\n\n

বাকি বেতনের কাপড়ে ব্যয়ের শতাংশ = 30%

\n\n

খরচের পর সঞ্চয় = 1,260 টাকা 

\n\n

অনুসৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ধরা যাক, মাসিক বেতন S টাকা।

\n\n

খাদ্যদ্রব্যে ব্যয়িত অর্থ = S এর 40% = 0.40S

\n\n

খাদ্যদ্রব্যে খরচের পর অবশিষ্ট বেতন = S - 0.40S = 0.60S

\n\n

\n\n

পরিবহনে খরচের পর অবশিষ্ট বেতন = 0.60S - 0.30S = 0.30S

\n\n

\n\n

কাপড়ে খরচের পর অবশিষ্ট বেতন = 0.30S - 0.09S = 0.21S

\n\n

⇒ S = 1,260 / 0.21

\n\n

⇒ S = 6,000

\n\n

মাসিক বেতন 6,000 টাকা।

ನೀ\nಡಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

ಆಹಾರದ ಮೇಲೆ ಖರ್ಚು ಮಾಡಿದ ಸಂಬಳದ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು = 40%

\n\n

ಉಳಿದ ಸಂಬಳದಲ್ಲಿ ಸಾರಿಗೆಗಾಗಿ ಖರ್ಚು ಮಾಡಿದ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು = 50%

\n\n

ಉಳಿದ ಸಂಬಳದಲ್ಲಿ ಬಟ್ಟೆಗಾಗಿ ಖರ್ಚು ಮಾಡಿದ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು = 30%

\n\n

ಖರ್ಚುಗಳ ನಂತರದ ಉಳಿತಾಯ = ₹1,260

\n\n

ಸೂತ್ರ ಬಳಸಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

ಸಾರಿಗೆಯ ನಂತರ ಉಳಿದ ಸಂಬಳ = 0.60S - 0.30S = 0.30S

\n\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டவை:

சம்பளத்தில் உணவிற்காக செலவிடப்பட்ட சதவீதம் = 40%

Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of Expenditure to Savings = 60 : 40 = 3 : 2

\n

Let the percentage increase in savings be x%.

\n

Using the weighted average method for percentage change: (3 × 20 + 2 × x) ÷ (3 + 2) = 30

\n

60 + 2x = 150 ⇒ 2x = 90

\n

x = 45%

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45%.

उपरोक्त में Y का मान रखने के बाद

\n\n

⇒ (49/100) × X × (1/100) × 50

\n\n

⇒ (2450/10000) × X

\n\n

⇒ 0.245 × X

\n\n

प्रतिशत के लिए हमें उपरोक्त संख्या को 100 से गुणा करना होगा

\n\n

⇒ 0.245 × X × 100

\n\n

⇒ 24.5% × X

\n\n

∴ 50 का Y%, X का 24.5% है।

\n34:T460,

\n\n

നൽകിയിരിക്കുന്നത്:

\n\n

X = Y യുടെ 49%

\n\n

കണക്കുകൂട്ടല്‍:

\n\n

X ന്റെ 49% = Y 

\n\n

⇒ Y = (49/100) × X

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ (2450/10000) × X

\n\n

⇒ 0.245 × X

Given:

\n\n

In a decade, a town's population grew from 1,75,000 to 2,62,500.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Percentage increase = \\(\\frac{\\text {present value - old value}}{\\text {old value}}\\)

\n\n

Average = \\(\\frac{\\text {sum of all observations}}{\\text {total number of observations}}\\)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Percentage increase in a decade = \\(\\frac{262500-175000}{175000}\\times 100=\\frac{87500}{1750}=50\\%\\) 

\n\n

Annual average = \\(\\frac{50}{10}=5\\%\\)

\n\n

∴  The average annual percentage increase in population is 5%.

Given:

\n\n

Time reduction = 25%

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Speed ∝ 1 / Time

\n\n

Percentage Increase in Speed = (New Speed - Old Speed) / Old Speed × 100%

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the original speed be S and ", "hn": "

दिया गया है:

\n\n

समय में कमी = 25%

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

चाल ∝ 1 / समय

\n\n

चाल में प्रतिशत वृद्धि = (नई चाल - पुरानी चाल) / पुरानी चाल × 100%

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि मूल चाल S है और मूल समय T है।

\n\n

नया समय = T - 0.25T = 0.75T

\n\n

चूँकि चाल ∝ 1 / समय:

\n\n

नई चाल = S / 0.75

\n\n

नई चाल = 4S / 3

\n\n

चाल में प्रतिशत वृद्धि = ((4S / 3) - S) / S × 100%

\n\n

⇒ चाल में प्रतिशत वृद्धि = (4/3 - 1) × 100%

\n\n

⇒ चाल में प्रतिशत वृद्धि = (1/3) × 100%

\n\n

⇒ चाल में प्रतिशत वृद्धि = 33 (1/3)%

\n\n

सही उत्तर विकल्प 4 है।

\n96:Tca0,\n

Shortcut Trick\n

If a value A is R% more than B, then B is less than A by: [R ÷ (100 + R)] × 100%

\n

Here, R = 2.5

\n

Required % = [2.5 ÷ ", "te": "

ఇచ్చినవి:

\n\n

సమయం తగ్గింపు = 25%

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

వేగం ∝ 1 / సమయం

\n\n

వేగంలో శాతం పెరుగుదల = (కొత్త వేగం - పాత వేగం) / పాత వేగం x 100%

\n\n

లెక్క:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

"solution_html": {"en": "\n

Given:

\n\n

The numerator is increased by 140% and denominator is decreased by 20%

\n\n

The resultant fraction is 12/7.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the original fraction be \\({x \\over y}%\\)

\n\n

According to the question,

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{x\\; + \\;140\\% \\;of\\;x\\;}}{{y\\;-\\;20\\% \\;of\\;y}}\\; = \\;\\frac{12}{7}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{12x}}{4y}\\; = \\;\\frac{12}{7}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\frac{x}{y}\\; = \\;\\frac{4}{{7}}\\)

\n\n

∴ Option 2 is the correct answer.

\n\n

"30%"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of Expenditure to Savings = 60 : 40 = 3 : 2

\n

Let the percentage increase in savings be x%.

\n

Using the weighted average method for percentage change: (3 × 20 + 2 × x) ÷ (3 + 2) = 30

\n

60 + 2x = 150 ⇒ 2x = 90

\n

x = 45%

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45%.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Ramesh spends 60% of his income.

\n

His income increases by 30%.

\n

His expenditure increases by 20%.

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Income = Expenditure + Savings

\n

Percentage Increase = (Increase Value ÷ Initial Value) × 100

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let Ramesh's initial income be 100.

\n

⇒ Initial expenditure = 60% of 100 = 60

\n

⇒ Initial savings = Income − Expenditure = 100 − 60 = 40

\n

⇒ New income = 100 + (30% of 100) = 130

\n

⇒ New expenditure = 60 + (20% of 60) = 60 + 12 = 72

\n

⇒ New savings = New income − New expenditure = 130 − 72 = 58

\n

⇒ Increase in savings = 58 − 40 = 18

\n

⇒ Percentage increase in savings = (Increase in savings ÷ Initial savings) × 100

\n

⇒ Percentage increase = (18 ÷ 40) × 100 = 45%

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45%.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Percentage Change Formula

\n

Percentage change = [(Final Value − Initial Value) ÷ Initial Value] × 100. A positive result indicates an increase, while a negative result implies a decrease.

\n

Weighted Average Method

\n

When a base value is split into two parts in the ratio m:n, and their respective percentage changes are a% and b%, the net percentage change is given by (ma + nb) ÷ (m + n).

\n

Successive Percentage Change

\n

If a value undergoes two successive percentage changes of a% and b%, the net percentage change is calculated as (a + b + ab ÷ 100)%.

ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ:

ರಮೇಶ್ ತಮ್ಮ ಆದಾಯದ ಶೇ.60ರಷ್ಟು ಖರ್ಚು ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾರೆ.

ಆದಾಯ ಹೆಚ್ಚಳ = 30%

ವೆಚ್ಚ ಹೆಚ್ಚಳ = 20%

ಬಳಸಿದ ಸೂತ್ರ:

ಉಳಿತಾಯ = ಆದಾಯ - ಖರ್ಚು

ಉಳಿತಾಯದಲ್ಲಿ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು ಹೆಚ್ಚಳ = \\(\\frac{New Savings - Old Savings}{Old Savings}\\) × 100

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಆದಾಯ 100 ಆಗಿರಲಿ.

ಆರಂಭಿಕ ವೆಚ್ಚ = 100 = 60 ರಲ್ಲಿ 60%

ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಉಳಿತಾಯ = 100 - 60 = 40

ಹೊಸ ಆದಾಯ = 100 + 30% ರಲ್ಲಿ 100 = 100 + 30 = 130

ಹೊಸ ಖರ್ಚು = 60 + 20% ರಲ್ಲಿ 60 = 60 + 12 = 72

ಹೊಸ ಉಳಿತಾಯ = 130 - 72 = 58

ಉಳಿತಾಯದಲ್ಲಿ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು ಹೆಚ್ಚಳ = [(58 - 40) / 40] × 100

⇒ (18 / 40) × 100

⇒ 0.45 × 100

⇒ 45%

∴ ರಮೇಶನ ಉಳಿತಾಯದ ಶೇಕಡಾವಾರು ಹೆಚ್ಚಳವು 45% ಆಗಿದೆ.

\n", "ta": "

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

ரமேஷ் தனது வருமானத்தில் 60% செலவிடுகிறார்.

வருமான அதிகரிப்பு = 30%

செலவு அதிகரிப்பு = 20%

பயன்படுத்தப்படும் சூத்திரம்:

சேமிப்பு = வருமானம் - செலவு

சேமிப்பில் சதவீதம் அதிகரிப்பு = \\(\\frac{New Savings - Old Savings}{Old Savings}\\) × 100

கணக்கீடு:

ஆரம்ப வருமானம் 100 ஆக இருக்கட்டும்.

ஆரம்ப செலவு = 100 = 60 இல் 60%

ஆரம்ப சேமிப்பு = 100 - 60 = 40

புதிய வருமானம் = 100 + 30% = 100 + 30 = 130

புதிய செலவு = 60 + 20% இல் 60 = 60 + 12 = 72

புதிய சேமிப்பு = 130 - 72 = 58

சேமிப்பில் சதவீதம் அதிகரிப்பு = [(58 - 40) / 40] × 100

⇒ (18 / 40) × 100

⇒ 0.45 × 100

⇒ 45%

∴ ரமேஷின் சேமிப்பில் சதவீதம் அதிகரிப்பு 45%.

\n", "or": "

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ରମେଶ ତାଙ୍କ ଆୟର 60% ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ କରନ୍ତି।

\n\n

ଆୟ ବୃଦ୍ଧି = 30%

\n\n

ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ ବୃଦ୍ଧି = 20%

\n\n

ସୂତ୍ର ବ୍ୟବହୃତ:

\n\n

ସଞ୍ଚୟ = ଆୟ - ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ

\n\n

ସଞ୍ଚୟରେ ଶତକଡା ବୃଦ୍ଧି = \\(\\frac{ନୂତନ ସଞ୍ଚୟ - ପୁରୁଣା ସଞ୍ଚୟ}{ପୁରୁଣା ସଞ୍ଚୟ}\\) x 100

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

ପ୍ରାରମ୍ଭିକ ଆୟ 100 ହେଉ।

\n\n

ପ୍ରାରମ୍ଭିକ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ = 100 ର 60% = 60

\n\n

ପ୍ରାରମ୍ଭିକ ସଞ୍ଚୟ = 100 - 60 = 40

\n\n

ନୂତନ ଆୟ = 100 + 100 ର 30% = 100 + 30 = 130

\n\n

ନୂତନ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ = 60 + 60 ର 20% = 60 + 12 = 72

\n\n

ନୂତନ ସଞ୍ଚୟ = 130 - 72 = 58

\n\n

ସଞ୍ଚୟରେ ଶତକଡା ବୃଦ୍ଧି = [(58 - 40) / 40] x 100

\n\n

Given

\n\n

Increase in price = 20%

\n\n

Decrease in consumption = 5%

\n\n

Concept:

\n\n

Percentage change in expenditure = Percentage change in price - Percentage change in consumption - (Percentage change in price × Percentage change in consumption) / 100

\n\n

Solution:

\n\n

Percentage change in expenditure = 20% - 5% - (20% × 5%)/100

\n\n

15% - 1% = 14%

\n\n

Therefore, the percentage change in total expenditure is 14%.

    \n\n\t

    ହଳ:

    \n\n\t

    ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚରେ ଶତକଡ଼ା ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ = 20% - 5% - (20% × 5%) / 100 |

    \n\n\t

    15% - 1% = 14% |

Given:

\n\n

The length of a rectangle is decreased by 11%

\n\n

The breadth of the rectangle is decreased by 11%

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Area of rectangle = Length × Breadth

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the length of the rectangle be L and breadth be B

\n\n

The initial area of rectangle = LB

\n\n

As the length of the rectangle is decreased by 11%

\n\n

Decreased length = L – 11% of L

\n\n

⇒ Decreased length = 0.89L

\n\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

आयत की लम्बाई में 11% की कमी होती है।

\n\n

आयत की चौड़ाई में 11% की कमी होती है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

आयत का क्षेत्रफल = लम्बाई × चौड़ाई

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

माना आयत की लम्बाई L और चौड़ाई B है।

\n\n

आयत का प्रारंभिक क्षेत्रफल = LB

\n\n

चूँकि, आयत की लम्बाई में 11% की कमी होती है

\n\n

कमी के बाद लम्बाई = L – L का 11%

\n\n

⇒ कमी के बाद लम्बाई = 0.89L

\n\n

चूँकि, आयत की चौड़ाई में 11% की कमी होती है

\n\n

कमी के बाद चौड़ाई = B – B का 11%

\n\n

⇒ कमी के बाद चौड़ाई = 0.89B

\n\n

आयत का नया क्षेत्रफल = 0.89L × 0.89B

\n\n

⇒ आयत का नया क्षेत्रफल = 0.7921LB

\n\n

क्षेत्रफल में कुल कमी = LB – 0.7921LB = 0.2079LB

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ क्षेत्रफल में प्रतिशत कमी = (0.2079LB/LB) × 100 = 20.79%

\n\n

∴ क्षेत्रफल में प्रतिशत कमी 20.79% है।

Given:

\n\n

Monthly salary of Jitvik in December 2020 = ₹24,800

\n\n

Annual salary increase = 5%

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

New Salary = Old Salary × (1 + Rate/100)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Salary in January 2021 = 24,800 × (1 + 5/100)

\n\n

⇒ Salary in January 2021 = 24,800 × 1.05

\n\n

⇒ Salary in January 2021 = 26,040

\n\n

Salary in January 2022 = 26,040 × (1 + 5/100)

\n\n

⇒ Salary in January 2022 = 26,040 × 1.05

\n\n

⇒ Salary in January 2022 = 27,342

\n\n

⇒ Salary in February 2022 = 27,342

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option 4.

\n\n

ਜਨਵਰੀ 2021 ਵਿੱਚ ਤਨਖਾਹ = 24,800 x (1 + 5/100)

\n\n

⇒ ਜਨਵਰੀ 2021 ਵਿੱਚ ਤਨਖਾਹ = 24,800 x 1.05

\n\n

⇒ ਜਨਵਰੀ 2021 ਵਿੱਚ ਤਨਖਾਹ = 26,040

\n\n

ਜਨਵਰੀ 2022 ਵਿੱਚ ਤਨਖਾਹ = 26,040 x (1 + 5/100)

\n\n

⇒ ਜਨਵਰੀ 2022 ਵਿੱਚ ਤਨਖਾਹ = 26,040 x 1.05

\n\n

⇒ ਜਨਵਰੀ 2022 ਵਿੱਚ ਤਨਖਾਹ = 27,342

\n\n

⇒ ਫਰਵਰੀ 2022 ਵਿੱਚ ਤਨਖਾਹ = 27,342

\n\n

∴ ਸਹੀ ਜਵਾਬ ਚੋਣ 4 ਹੈ।

Calculation:

\n\n

20% of a = b

\n\n

⇒ 20/100 × a = b      ----(1)

\n\n

b% of 20 = b/100 × 20

\n\n

Put the value of b from equation (1) into the above expression,

\n\n

⇒ b/100 × 20 = 20/100 × a × 20/100

\n\n

= 4/100 × a = 4% of a

Given:

\n\n

Price of the electric bike last year = Rs. 1,25,000

\n\n

Decrease in price = 30%

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Price this year = Last year's price - (Decrease percentage × Last year's price)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Decrease in price = 30% of 1,25,000

\n\n

⇒ Decrease in price = (30/100) × 1,25,000

\n\n

⇒ Decrease in price = 0.30 × 1,25,000

\n\n

⇒ Decrease in price = 37,500

\n\n

Price this year = Last year's price - Decrease in price

\n\n

⇒ Price this year = 1,25,000 - 37,500

\n\n

⇒ Price this year = 87,500

\n\n

The price of the electric bike this year is Rs. 87,500.

Given:

\n\n

The price of a watch is reduced to 20%

\n\n

The number of watches sold has increased by 40%

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

effective percentage change =  x + y + (xy/100)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Since first it is reduced to 20% and then it has increased by 40%

\n\n

Effective percentage  change = - x + y - (xy/100) 

\n\n

⇒ -20 +40 - 800/100 =  +12

\n\n

Here + sign shows an increase in the percentage

\n\n

∴ The effect on sales in percentage is a 12% increase.

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

{"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "

Given:

\n\n

Initial expenditure of Ritvik = ₹12,800

\n\n

New expenditure of Ritvik = ₹11,712

\n\n

Concept Used:

\n\n

Percentage decrease in expenditure is calculated using the formula:

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Percentage Decrease = ((Initial Expenditure - New Expenditure) / Initial Expenditure) × 100

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

We have,

\n\n

⇒ Initial Expenditure = ₹12,800

\n\n

⇒ New Expenditure = ₹11,712

\n\n

⇒ Decrease in Expenditure = ₹12,800 - ₹11,712

\n\n

⇒ Decrease in Expenditure = ₹1,088

\n\n

⇒ Percentage Decrease = (₹1,0\n88 / ₹12,800) × 100

\n\n

⇒ Percentage Decrease = 0.085 × 100

\n\n

⇒ Percentage Decrease = 8.5%

\n\n

∴ The percentage decrease in Ritvik's expenditure is 8.5%.

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਸੰਕਲਪ:

\n\n

ਖਰਚੇ ਵਿੱਚ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਕਮੀ ਦੀ ਗਣਨਾ ਹੇਠਲੇ ਫਾਰਮੂਲੇ ਦੀ ਵਰਤੋਂ ਕਰਕੇ ਕੀਤੀ ਜਾਂਦੀ ਹੈ:

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਸੂਤਰ :

\n\n

ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਕਮੀ = ((ਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ ਖਰਚਾ - ਨਵਾਂ ਖਰਚਾ) / ਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ ਖਰਚਾ) x 100

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

ਸਾਡੇ ਕੋਲ ਹੈ,

\n\n

⇒ ਸ਼ੁਰੂਆਤੀ ਖਰਚਾ = ₹12,800

\n\n

⇒ ਨਵਾਂ ਖਰਚਾ = ₹11,712

\n\n

⇒ ਖਰਚੇ ਵਿੱਚ ਕਮੀ = ₹12,800 - ₹11,712

\n\n

⇒ ਖਰਚੇ ਵਿੱਚ ਕਮੀ = ₹1,088

\n\n

⇒ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਕਮੀ = (₹1,088 / ₹12,800) x 100

\n\n

⇒ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਕਮੀ = 0.085 x 100

\n\n

⇒ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਕਮੀ = 8.5%

\n\n

∴ ਰਿਤਵਿਕ ਦੇ ਖਰਚੇ ਵਿੱਚ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਕਮੀ 8.5% ਹੈ।

Given:

\n\n

The cost of rice is increased by 25%.

\n\n

Its consumption is decreased by 30%.

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Percentage change in expenditure = (Change in cost) × (Change in consumption)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the original cost of rice be 100 units and the original consumption be 100 units.

\n\n

New cost of rice = 100 + 25% of 100

\n\n

New cost of rice = 100 + 25

\n\n

New cost of rice = 125 units

\n\n

व्यय में प्रतिशत परिवर्तन = (मूल्य में परिवर्तन) × (खपत में परिवर्तन)

\n\n

ఇవ్వబడింది:

\n\n

వరి ధర 25% పెరిగింది.

\n\n

దాని వినియోగం 30% తగ్గింది.

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

ఖర్చులో శాతం మార్పు = (ధరలో మార్పు) x (వినియోగంలో మార్పు)

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

వరి యొక్క అసలు ధర 100 యూనిట్లు మరియు అసలు వినియోగం 100 యూనిట్లు అని అనుకుందాం.

\n\n

వరి యొక్క కొత్త ధర = 100 + 100లో 25%

\n\n

వరి యొక్క కొత్త ధర = 100 + 25

\n\n

వరి యొక్క కొత్త ధర = 125 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

వరి యొక్క కొత్త వినియోగం = 100 - 100లో 30%

\n\n

వరి యొక్క కొత్త వినియోగం = 100 - 30

\n\n

వరి యొక్క కొత్త వినియోగం = 70 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

అసలు ఖర్చు = 100 x 100

\n\n

అసలు ఖర్చు = 10000 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

కొత్త ఖర్చు = 125 x 70

\n\n

కొత్త ఖర్చు = 8750 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

ఖర్చులో శాతం మార్పు

\n\n

⇒ (అసలు ఖర్చు - కొత్త ఖర్చు) / (అసలు ఖర్చు) x 100

\n\n

ఖర్చులో శాతం మార్పు

\n\n

⇒ (10000 - 8750) / (10000) x 100

\n\n

ఖర్చులో శాతం మార్పు

\n\n

⇒ 1250 / 10000 x 100

\n\n

ఖర్చులో శాతం మార్పు

\n\n

⇒ 12.5%

\n\n

డబ్బు ఖర్చు 12(1/2)% తగ్గింది.

চালের ক্রয় মূল্য 25% বৃদ্ধি পেয়েছে।

প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

চালের ক্রয় মূল্য 25% বৃদ্ধি পেয়েছে।

\n\n

এর খরচ 30% কমে গেছে।

\n\n

ব্যবহৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

ব্যয়ের শতকরা পরিবর্তন = (দামের পরিবর্তন) × (খরচের পরিবর্তন)

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

ধরুন, চালের আসল দাম 100 ইউনিট এবং আসল খরচ 100 ইউনিট।

\n\n

চালের নতুন দাম = 100 + 100 এর 25%

\n\n

চালের নতুন দাম = 100 + 25

\n\n

চালের নতুন দাম = 125 ইউনিট

\n\n

চালের নতুন খরচ = 100 - 100 এর 30%

\n\n

চালের নতুন খরচ = 100 - 30

\n\n

ধানের নতুন খরচ = 70 ইউনিট

\n\n

আসল ব্যয় = 100 × 100

\n\n

আসল ব্যয় = 1", "ml": "", "gu": "

આપેલ:

\n\n

ચોખાની કિંમત 25% વધે છે.

\n\n

તેનો વપરાશ 30% ઘટે છે.

\n\n

વપરાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

ખર્ચમાં ટકાવારી ફેરફાર = (કિંમતમાં ફેરફાર) x (વપરાશમાં ફેરફાર)

\n\n

ગણતરી:

\n\n

ધારો કે ચોખાની મૂળ કિંમત 100 એકમ છે અને મૂળ વપરાશ 100 એકમ છે.

\n\n

ચોખાની નવી કિંમત = 100 + 100 ના 25%

\n\n

ચોખાની નવી કિંમત = 100 + 25

\n\n

ચોખાની નવી કિંમત = 125 એકમ

\n\n

ચોખાનો નવો વપરાશ = 100 - 100 ના 30%

\n\n

ચોખાનો નવો વપરાશ = 100 - 30

\n\n

ચોખાનો નવો વપરાશ = 70 એકમ

\n\n

મૂળ ખર્ચ = 100 x 100

\n\n

મૂળ ખર્ચ = 10000 એકમ

\n\n

નવો ખર્ચ = 125 x 70

\n\n

નવો ખર્ચ = 8750 એકમ

\n\n

ખર્ચમાં ટકાવારી ફેરફાર

\n\n

⇒ (મૂળ ખર્ચ - નવો ખર્ચ) / (મૂળ ખર્ચ) x 100

\n\n

ખર્ચમાં ટકાવારી ફેરફાર

\n\n

⇒ (10000 - 8750) / (10000) x 100

\n\n

ખર્ચમાં ટકાવારી ફેરફાર

\n\n

⇒ 1250 / 10000 x 100

\n\n

ખર્ચમાં ટકાવારી ફેરફાર

\n\n

⇒ 12.5%

\n\n

પૈસાનો ખર્ચ 12(1/2)% ઘટ્યો.

\n\n

ಅದರ ಬಳಕೆ 30% ಕಡಿಮೆಯಾಗಿದೆ.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ಅಕ್ಕಿಯ ಮೂಲ ಬೆಲೆ 100 ಯೂನಿಟ್‌ಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಮೂಲ ಬಳಕೆ 100 ಯೂನಿಟ್‌ಗಳು ಎಂದು ಭಾವಿಸೋಣ.

\n\n

ಅಕ್ಕಿಯ ಹೊಸ ಬೆಲೆ = 100 + 100 ರ 25%

\n\n

ಅಕ್ಕಿಯ ಹೊಸ ಬೆಲೆ = 100 + 25

\n\n

ಅಕ್ಕಿಯ ಹೊಸ ಬೆಲೆ = 125 ಯೂನಿಟ್‌ಗಳು

\n\n

ಅಕ್ಕಿಯ ಹೊಸ ಬಳಕೆ = 100 - 100 ರ 30%

\n\n

ಅಕ್ಕಿಯ ಹೊಸ ಬಳಕೆ = 100 - 30

\n\n

ಅಕ್ಕಿಯ ಹೊಸ ಬಳಕೆ = 70 ಯೂನಿಟ್‌ಗಳು

\n\n

ಮೂಲ ವೆಚ್ಚ = 100 x 100

\n\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n\n

அரிசியின் விலை 25% அதிகரிக்கிறது.

\n\n

அதன் நுகர்வு 30% குறைகிறது.

\n\n

பயன்படுத்தப்பட்ட சூத்திரம்:

\n\n

செலவில் ஏற்படும் சதவீத மாற்றம் = (விலையில் ஏற்படும் மாற்றம்) x (நுகர்வில் ஏற்படும் மாற்றம்)

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

அரிசியின் அசல் விலை 100 அலகுகள் மற்றும் அசல் நுகர்வு 100 அலகுகள் என வைத்துக்கொள்வோம்.

\n\n

அரிசியின் புதிய விலை = 100 + 100 இன் 25%

\n\n

அரிசியின் புதிய விலை = 100 + 25

\n\n

அரிசியின் புதிய விலை = 125 அலகுகள்

\n\n

அரிசியின் புதிய நுகர்வு = 100 - 100 இன் 30%

\n\n

அரிசியின் புதிய நுகர்வு = 100 - 30

\n\n

அரிசியின் புதிய நுகர்வு = 70 அலகுகள்

\n\n

அசல் செலவு = 100 x 100

\n\n

அசல் செலவு = 10000 அலகுகள்

ପ୍ରଦତ୍ତ:

\n\n

ଚାଉଳର ମୂଲ୍ୟ 25% ବୃଦ୍ଧି ପାଇଛି।

\n\n

ଏହାର ବ୍ୟବହାର 30% ହ୍ରାସ ପାଇଛି।

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚରେ ଶତକଡ଼ା ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ = (ମୂଲ୍ୟରେ ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ) x (ବ୍ୟବହାରରେ ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ)

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

ଧରନ୍ତୁ ଚାଉଳର ପ୍ରକୃତ ମୂଲ୍ୟ 100 ୟୁନିଟ୍ ଏବଂ ପ୍ରକୃତ ବ୍ୟବହାର 100 ୟୁନିଟ୍।

\n\n

ଚାଉଳର ନୂତନ ମୂଲ୍ୟ = 100 + 100 ର 25%

\n\n

ଚାଉଳର ନୂତନ ମୂଲ୍ୟ = 100 + 25

\n\n

ଚାଉଳର ନୂତନ ମୂଲ୍ୟ = 125 ୟୁନିଟ୍

\n\n

ଚାଉଳର ନୂତନ ବ୍ୟବହାର = 100 - 100 ର 30%

\n\n

ଚାଉଳର ନୂତନ ବ୍ୟବହାର = 100 - 30

\n\n

ଚାଉଳର ନୂତନ ବ୍ୟବହାର = 70 ୟୁନିଟ୍

\n\n

ପ୍ରକୃତ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ = 100 x 100

\n\n

ପ୍ରକୃତ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ = 10000 ୟୁନିଟ୍

\n\n

ନୂତନ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ = 125 x 70

\n\n

ନୂତନ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ = 8750 ୟୁନିଟ୍

\n\n

ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚରେ ଶତକଡ଼ା ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ

\n\n

⇒ (ପ୍ରକୃତ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ - ନୂତନ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ) / (ପ୍ରକୃତ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ) x 100

\n\n

ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚରେ ଶତକଡ଼ା \nପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ

\n\n

⇒ (10000 - 8750) / (10000) x 100

\n\n

ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚରେ ଶତକଡ଼ା ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ

\n\n

⇒ 1250 / 10000 x 100

\n\n

ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚରେ ଶତକଡ଼ା ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ

\n\n

⇒ 12.5%

\n\n

ଟଙ୍କା ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ 12(1/2)% ହ୍ରାସ ପାଇଲା।

\n\n

ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚରେ ଶତକଡ଼ା ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ

\n\n

⇒ (ପ୍ରକୃତ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ - ନୂତନ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ) / (ପ୍ରକୃତ ଖର୍ଚ୍ଚ) x ", "as": "", "ks": "", "kok": "", "mni": "", "ne": "", "pa": "

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ:

\n\n

ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਕੀਮਤ 25% ਵਧ ਗਈ ਹੈ।

\n\n

ਇਸਦੀ ਖਪਤ 30% ਘੱਟ ਗਈ ਹੈ।

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਫਾਰਮੂਲਾ:

\n\n

ਖਰਚੇ ਵਿੱਚ ਪ੍ਰਤੀਸ਼ਤ ਬਦਲਾਅ = (ਕੀਮਤ ਵਿੱਚ ਬਦਲਾਅ) x (ਖਪਤ ਵਿੱਚ ਬਦਲਾਅ)

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

ਮੰਨ ਲਓ ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਅਸਲ ਕੀਮਤ 100 ਯੂਨਿਟ ਹੈ ਅਤੇ ਅਸਲ ਖਪਤ 100 ਯੂਨਿਟ ਹੈ।

\n\n

ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਨਵੀਂ ਕੀਮਤ = 100 + 100 ਦਾ 25%

\n\n

ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਨਵੀਂ ਕੀਮਤ = 100 + 25

\n\n

ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਨਵੀਂ ਕੀਮਤ = 125 ਯੂਨਿਟ

\n\n

ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਨਵੀਂ ਖਪਤ = 100 - 100 ਦਾ 30%

\n\n

ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਨਵੀਂ ਖਪਤ = 100 - 30

\n\n

ਚੌਲਾਂ ਦੀ ਨਵੀਂ ਖਪਤ = 70 ਯੂਨਿਟ

\n\n

ਅਸਲ ਖਰਚਾ = 100 x 100

\n\n

ਅਸਲ ਖਰਚਾ = 10000 ਯੂਨਿਟ

\n\n

ਨਵਾਂ ਖਰਚਾ = 125 x 70

\n\n

ਨਵਾਂ ਖਰਚਾ = 8750 ਯੂਨਿਟ

\n\n

Given: 

\n\n

Rotten apples = 10%

\n\n

Remaining sold good apples = 85%

\n\n

good apples = 405

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

let he had originally be X apples.

\n\n

According to the question:

\n\n

∴ Rotten apples = (X × 10%) =10X/100

\n\n

⇒ Rotten apples = X/10

\n\n

Again,

\n\n

sold apples = 85% 0f (X – X/10)

\n\n

⇒ [(85/100) × (9X/10)] = 153X/200

\n\n

Now,

\n\n

(X/10 + 153X/200 + 405) = X

\n\n

⇒ (20X + 153X + 81000)/200 = X

\n\n

⇒ 173X + 81000 = 200X

\n\n

⇒ 200X – 173X = 81000

\n\n

⇒ 27X = 81000

\n\n

⇒ X = 81000/27

\n\n

⇒ X = 3000

\n\n

∴ He originally had 3000 apples.

Given:

\n\n

Decrease in production of company = 35%

\n\n

Concept Used:

\n\n

Total Work = Manpower/Production × Working Hours

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the total number of men be 100 and the working hours be 10 hours.

\n\n

⇒ Total work = 100 × 10 = 1000 units

\n\n

⇒ Men left after decrease = 100 – 35% of 100 = 100 – \\(\\frac{35}{100}\\) × 100

\n\n

⇒ Men left after decrease = 100 – 35 = 65

\n\n

After the decrease, let the working hours be x.

\n\n

Equating with the total work, we get

\n\n

⇒ 65 × x = 10000

\n\n

⇒ x = \\(\\frac{10000}{65}\\) = 153.85 hours

\n\n

⇒ Working hours should increase by percent = 153.85 – 100 = 53.85%

\n\n

Therefore, the working hours should be increased by 53.85% to restore the original production.

GIVEN:

\n\n

Population = 80,000

\n\n

population decreases in the 1st year = 8%

\n\n

population increases in the 2nd year = 5%

\n\n

FORMULA USED:

\n\n

New value = ( 100 + X)/100 × Actual value , where X = percent by which value is increased.

\n\n

New value = ( 100 - X)/100 × Actual value , where X = percent by which value is decreased.

\n\n

CALCULATION:

\n\n

Population of town after 1st year = 80,000 - 80,000 × 8 /100 = (80,000- 6,400) = 73,600

\n\n

Population of town after 2nd year = 73,600 + 73,600 × 5/100 = ( 73,600 + 3,680) = 77,280

\n\n

∴ The population at the end of 2 years is 77,280.

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

नवीन मूल्य = ( 100 + X)/100 × वास्तविक मूल्य , जेथे X = टक्के ज्याने मूल्य व", "mr": "\n

दिलेल्याप्रमाणे:

\n\n

लोकसंख्या = 80,000

\n\n

पहिल्या वर्षी घटलेली लोकसंख्या = 8%

\n\n

दुसऱ्या वर्षी वाढलेली लोकसंख्या = 5%

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

नवीन मूल्य = ( 100 + X)/100 × वास्तविक मूल्य , जेथे X = टक्के ज्याने मूल्य वाढविले आहे.

\n\n

नवीन मूल्य = ( 100 - X)/100 × वास्तविक मूल्य, जेथे X = टक्के ज्याने मूल्य कमी केले आहे.

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

पहिल्या वर्षानंतर शहराची लोकसंख्या = 80,000 - 80,000 × 8/100 = (80,000- 6,400) = 73,600

\n\n

दुसऱ्या वर्षानंतर शहराची लोकसंख्या = 73,600 + 73,600 × 5/100 = ( 73,600 + 3,680) = 77,280

\n\n

∴ 2 वर्षांच्या शेवटी लोकसंख्या 77,280 आहे.

\n\n

Alternate Method
\nगणना:

\n\n\n\t\n\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\n\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\n\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\n\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\n\t\t\n\t\n
 मागील वर्षपुढील वर्षी
8% ची घट10092
5% ची वाढ2021
एकूणच20001932
\n\n

आता,

\n\n

2000 एकक = 80000

\n\n

1 एकक = 40

\n\n

1932 एकक = 40 × 1932 = 77280

\n\n

∴ योग्य उत्तर 77280 आहे.

\n\n4d:T48a,

Given:

\n\n

In a decade, a town's population grew from 1,75,000 to 2,62,500.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Percentage increase = \\(\\frac{\\text {present value - old value}}{\\text {old value}}\\)

\n\n

Average = \\(\\frac{\\text {sum of all observations}}{\\text {total number of observations}}\\)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Percentage increase in a decade = \\(\\frac{262500-175000}{175000}\\times 100=\\frac{87500}{1750}=50\\%\\)

Given:

\n\n

A number is first decreased by 18% and the resulting number is then increased by 25%.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Overall Percentage Change = (Final Value - Initial Value) / Initial Value × 100

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the original number be 100.

\n\n

After 18% decrease:

\n\n

⇒ 100 - 18 = 82

\n\n

After 25% increase on 82:

\n\n

⇒ 82 + (25% of 82)

\n\n

⇒ 82 + 20.5 = 102.5

\n\n

Overall Percentage Change:

\n\n

⇒ (102.5 - 100) / 100 × 100

\n\n

⇒ 2.5%

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option (4).

\n", "hn": "

दिया गया है:

\n\n

एक संख्या को पहले 18% कम किया जाता है और परिणामी संख्या को फिर 25% बढ़ाया जाता है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

कुल प्रतिशत परिवर्तन = (अंतिम मान - प्रारंभिक मान) / प्रारंभिक मान × 100

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि मूल संख्या 100 है।

\n\n

18% कम होने के बाद:

\n\n

⇒ 100 - 18 = 82

\n\n

82 पर 25% बढ़ने के बाद:

\n\n

⇒ 82 + (82 का 25%)

\n\n

⇒ 82 + 20.5 = 102.5

\n\n

कुल प्रतिशत परिवर्तन:

\n\n

⇒ (102.5 - 100) / 100 × 100

\n\n

⇒ 2.5%

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर विकल्प (4) है।

\n86:T67e,

दिया गया है:

\n\n

दिसंबर 2020 में जित्विक का मासिक वेतन = ₹24,800

\n\n

वार्षिक वेतन वृद्धि = 5%

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

नया वेतन = पुराना वेतन × (1 + दर/100)

\n\n

गणना:ఇచ్చినవి:

\n\n

ఒక సంఖ్యను మొదట 18% తగ్గించి, ఫలిత సంఖ్యను తరువాత 25% పెంచారు.

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

మొత్తం శాతం మార్పు = (చివరి విలువ - ప్రారంభ విలువ) / ప్రారంభ విలువ x 100

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

అసలు సంఖ్య 100 అని అనుకుందాం.

\n\n

18% తగ్గించిన తర్వాత:

\n\n

⇒ 100 - 18 = 82

\n\n

82 పై 25% పెంచిన తర్వాత:

\n\n

⇒ 82 + (82 లో 25%)

\n\n

⇒ 82 + 20.5 = 102.5

\n\n

మొత్తం శాతం మార్పు:

\n\n

⇒ (102.5 - 100) / 100 x 100

\n\n

⇒ 2.5%

\n\n

∴ సరైన సమాధానం ఎంపిక (4).

\n", "mr": "

दिले:

प्रथम संख्या 18% ने कमी केली जाते आणि परिणामी संख्या 25% ने वाढविली जाते.

वापरलेले सूत्र:

एकूण टक्केवारी बदल = (अंतिम मूल्य - आरंभिक मूल्य) / आरंभिक मूल्य × 100

गणना:

मूळ संख्या 100 असू द्या.

18% कमी झाल्यानंतर:

⇒ 100 - 18 = 82

82 वर 25% वाढ झाल्यानंतर:

⇒ ८२ + (८२ पैकी २५%)

⇒ ८२ + २०.५ = १०२.५

एकूण टक्केवारीतील बदल:

⇒ (102.5 - 100) / 100 × 100

⇒ २.५%

∴ योग्य उत्तर पर्याय (4) आहे.

Given:

\n\n

The monthly income of an employee was Rs. 26,500 and his monthly expenditure was Rs. 22,000.

\n\n

Next year his income increased by 20% and his expenditure increased by 15%.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Savings = Income - Expenses

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

According to the question, 

\n\n

Savings of the employee in initial year = 26500 - 22000 = Rs. 4,500

\n\n

Increase in income from next year, 

\n\n

I = 26500 (1 + 20%)

\n\n

⇒ I = 26500 (1 + 0.2)

\n\n

⇒ I = 26500 (1.2) = Rs. 31,800

\n\n

Increase in expenses from next year, 

\n\n

E = 22000 (1 + 15%)

\n\n

⇒ E = 22000 (1 + 0.15)

\n\n

⇒ E = 22000 (1.15) = Rs. 25,300

\n\n

Savings from next year,

\n\n

S = 31800 - 25300 = Rs. 6,500

\n\n

Savings increased by = 6500 - 4500

\n\n

⇒ Rs. 2,000

\n\n

The total increase in savings,

\n\n

S% = \\(\\frac{2000}{4500}\\)× 100

\n\n

⇒ S% = \\(\\frac{2000}{45}\\)

\n\n

⇒ S% = \\(\\frac{400}{9}\\) = 44\\(\\frac{4}{9}\\)%

\n\n

∴ The percentage increase in his savings is 44\\(\\frac{4}{9}\\)%.

Given:

\n\n

20% of P + 40% of Q = 80% of Q

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Percentages can be expressed as fractions or decimals. For example, 20% can be written as 0.2 or 1/5.

\n\n

Solution:

\n\n

20% of P + 40% of Q = 80% of Q

\n\n

0.2P + 0.4Q = 0.8Q

\n\n

0.2P = 0.8Q - 0.4Q

\n\n

0.2P = 0.4Q

\n\n

Now we can solve for P/Q:

\n\n

P/Q = 0.4 / 0.2

\n\n

P/Q = 2

\n\n

This tells us that P is equal to 2Q.

\n\n

 P is 200% of Q.

\n", "hn": "

दिया गया है:

\n\n

P का 20% + Q का 40% = Q का 80%

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

प्रतिशत को भिन्न या दशमलव के रूप में व्यक्त किया जा सकता है। उदाहरण के लिए, 20% को 0.2 या 1/5 के रूप में लिखा जा सकता है।

\n\n

हल:

\n\n

P का 20% + Q का 40% = Q का 80%

\n\n

0.2P + 0.4Q = 0.8Q

\n\n

0.2P = 0.8Q - 0.4Q

\n\n

0.2P = 0.4Q

\n\n

अब हम P/Q के लिए हल कर सकते हैं:

\n\n

P/Q = 0.4 / 0.2

\n\n

P/Q = 2

\n\n

यह हमें बताता है कि P, 2Q के बराबर है।

\n\n

  P, Q का 200% है।

Shortcut Trick\n

Let initial Income = 100, which means Savings = 25 and Expenditure = 75.

\n

New Income = 120 (increased by 20%) and Savings = 25 (remains constant).

\n

New Expenditure = 120 − 25 = 95.

\n

Increase in Expenditure = 95 − 75 = 20.

\n

Percentage Increase = (20 ÷ 75) × 100 = 26 2/3%.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 26 2/3%.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

A person saves 25% of his income.

\n

Income increases by 20% while savings remain the same.

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Income = Expenditure + Savings

\n

Percentage Increase = (Increase in Value ÷ Original Value) × 100

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the initial income be 100 units.

\n

⇒ Initial saving = 25% of 100 = 25 units

\n

⇒ Initial expenditure = 100 − 25 = 75 units

\n

⇒ After a 20% increase, the new income = 100 + 20% of 100 = 120 units

\n

⇒ New saving = 25 units (as it remains the same)

\n

⇒ New expenditure = 120 − 25 = 95 units

\n

⇒ Increase in expenditure = 95 − 75 = 20 units

\n

⇒ Percentage increase in expenditure = (20 ÷ 75) × 100

\n

⇒ Percentage increase = (4 ÷ 15) × 100 = 80 ÷ 3 = 26 2/3%

\n

∴ The correct answer is 26 2/3%.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Income-Expenditure Concept

\n

The foundational equation is Income = Expenditure + Savings. Any change in income will exactly equal the sum of changes in expenditure and savings.

\n

Constant Variable Effect

\n

If savings remain constant, the entire absolute increase in income directly translates to the absolute increase in expenditure.

\n

Percentage Change Formula

\n

Percentage change is always calculated based on the initial value: % Change = [(Final − Initial) ÷ Initial] × 100.

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

Total faculty = 750

\n\n

Percentage of females = 60%

\n\n

Average height of females = 162 cm

\n\n

Average height of males = 168 cm

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Average height of faculty = (Total height of females + Total height of males) / Total faculty

\n\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

कुल शिक्षक (फैकल्टी) = 750

\n\n

महिलाओं का प्रतिशत = 60%

\n\n

महिलाओं की औसत ऊँचाई = 162 सेमी

\n\n

पुरुषों की औसत ऊँचाई = 168 सेमी

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

शिक्षकों की औसत ऊँचाई = (महिलाओं की कुल ऊँचाई + पुरुषों की कुल ऊँचाई) / कुल  शिक्षक (फैकल्टी)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

महिलाओं की संख्या = 750 का 60% = 0.60 × 750 = 450

\n\n

पुरुषों की संख्या = 750 - 450 = 300

\n\n

महिलाओं की कुल ऊँचाई = 450 × 162 = 72900 सेमी

\n\n

पुरुषों की कुल ऊँचाई = 300 × 168 = 50400 सेमी

\n\n

⇒ फैकल्टी की औसत ऊँचाई = (72900 + 50400) / 750

\n\n

⇒ फैकल्टी की औसत ऊँचाई = 123300 / 750

\n\n

⇒ फैकल्टी की औसत ऊँचाई = 164.4 सेमी

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर विकल्प 4 है।

Given:

\n\n

Ramesh's pocket money is first reduced by 25% and then increased by 20%.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Final value = Initial value × (1 - Reduction%) × (1 + Increase%)

\n\n

Net percentage change = ((Final value - Initial value) / Initial value) × 100

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the original pocket money be ₹100.

\n\n

Af\nter a 25% reduction:

\n\n
